Total columns (J) should equal the last cumulative value\n    na_ws[f\"J{cumul_real_row}\"] = f\"=I{cumul_real_row}\"\n    na_ws[f\"J{cumul_nominal_row}\"] = f\"=I{cumul_nominal_row}\"\n\nprint(\"\\nCumulative formulas set up\")\n\n# Now also add Total column formulas for other rows\n# Total should be SUM across years for non-cumulative rows\n\nfor scenario_name, invest_row, _ in scenario_configs:\n    for item_offset in range(0, 6):  # 0=invest, 1=gel, 2=real, 3=nominal, 4=imports, 5=domestic\n        row = invest_row + item_offset\n        na_ws[f\"J{row}\"] = f\"=SUM(C{row}:I{row})\"\n\nprint(\"Total column formulas added\")\n\nwb.save(\"/root/test - demand.xlsx\")\nprint(\"Cumulative calculations and totals completed\")\n\nEOF\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1364,"cache_read_input_tokens":56746,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1364,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"445b2956-eea4-4a26-8a51-8c5a638e1e9e","uuid":"b4b6ea4a-f3cc-4af4-aa48-a5602dc52531"}
